## `AGO-2512-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** RT-Radiographic Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Pressure Vessel Hydrostatic Proof Test & Helium Mass Spectrometer Leak Detector
- **Intro:** RT-Radiographic Metrologist — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Pressure Vessel Hydrostatic Proof Test & Helium Mass Spectrometer Leak Detector — Pre-Job Safety Briefing & Equipment Verification: Calibration certificates must be within 90 days of issue. Ambient temperature must be 20°C ± 2°C. Relative humidity must be ≤60%.
- **Key tolerance:** Calibration certificates must be within 90 days of issue. Ambient temperature must be 20°C ± 2°C. Relative humidity must be ≤60%.
- **Standards:** `ISO 9712:2021`, `ISO 10012:2003`, `ASME Boiler and Pressure Vessel Code (BPVC) Section V - Nondestr`, `Division 1`
